### [54] [MotionBricks: Scalable Real-Time Motions with Modular Latent Generative Model and Smart Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24833)
*Tingwu Wang,Olivier Dionne,Michael De Ruyter,David Minor,Davis Rempe,Kaifeng Zhao,Mathis Petrovich,Ye Yuan,Chenran Li,Zhengyi Luo,Brian Robison,Xavier Blackwell,Bernardo Antoniazzi,Xue Bin Peng,Yuke Zhu,Simon Yuen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionBricks是一个大规模、实时生成式动作框架，通过模块化潜空间生成骨干和智能原语，解决了生成式动作合成在实时可扩展性和多模态集成方面的挑战，在开源和专有数据集上实现了SOTA质量（15,000 FPS，2ms延迟），并成功部署于动画和机器人控制领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式动作合成方法在实时交互控制中存在两大问题：1）实时可扩展性差——工业应用需要实时生成大量动作技能，但现有生成方法在实时计算约束下质量与可扩展性显著下降；2）集成困难——工业应用需要细粒度多模态控制（速度指令、风格选择、精确关键帧），而现有文本或标签驱动模型无法满足这些需求。

Method: 1）提出大规模模块化潜空间生成骨干，专为鲁棒实时动作生成设计，单个模型可建模超过35万个动作片段；2）引入智能原语（smart primitives），提供统一、鲁棒、直观的接口，支持导航和物体交互的创作，以即插即用的方式设计应用，无需专业知识。

Result: 在多种规模的开源和专有数据集上达到SOTA动作质量；实时吞吐量达15,000 FPS，延迟仅2ms；在完整的生产级动画演示中展示了统一模型下多种风格的导航和物体-场景交互；成功部署于Unitree G1人形机器人实现实时机器人控制。

Conclusion: MotionBricks有效弥合了生成式动作合成研究与工业应用之间的差距，在实时性、可扩展性、多模态控制和易用性方面均取得了显著突破，并可推广至动画之外的机器人控制领域。

Abstract: Despite transformative advances in generative motion synthesis, real-time interactive motion control remains dominated by traditional techniques. In this work, we identify two key challenges in bridging research and production: 1) Real-time scalability: Industry applications demand real-time generation of a vast repertoire of motion skills, while generative methods exhibit significant degradation in quality and scalability under real-time computation constraints, and 2) Integration: Industry applications demand fine-grained multi-modal control involving velocity commands, style selection, and precise keyframes, a need largely unmet by existing text- or tag-driven models. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MotionBricks: a large-scale, real-time generative framework with a two-fold solution. First, we propose a large-scale modular latent generative backbone tailored for robust real-time motion generation, effectively modeling a dataset of over 350,000 motion clips with a single model. Second, we introduce smart primitives that provide a unified, robust, and intuitive interface for authoring both navigation and object interaction. Applications can be designed in a plug-and-play manner like assembling bricks without expert animation knowledge. Quantitatively, we show that MotionBricks produces state-of-the-art motion quality on open-source and proprietary datasets of various scales, while also achieving a real-time throughput of 15,000 FPS with 2ms latency. We demonstrate the flexibility and robustness of MotionBricks in a complete production-level animation demo, covering navigation and object-scene interaction across various styles with a unified model. To showcase our framework's application beyond animation, we deploy MotionBricks on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot to demonstrate its flexibility and generalization for real-time robotic control.

</details>


### [55] [VISION-SLS: Safe Perception-Based Control from Learned Visual Representations via System Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24894)
*Antoine P. Leeman,Shuyu Zhan,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISION-SLS是一种面向高分辨率RGB图像的鲁棒非线性输出反馈控制方法，通过结合预训练视觉特征的低维观测映射与系统级综合（SLS）的因果仿射时变输出反馈策略，在部分可观测、传感器噪声和非线性动力学条件下提供约束满足保证。该方法采用序贯凸规划与Riccati递归的高效求解器处理非凸优化问题，在4D车辆、10D四旋翼飞行器和59D人形机器人任务以及真实硬件平台上验证了安全性与求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SLS的输出反馈控制方法在高维视觉输入下难以扩展，同时部分可观测性、传感器噪声和非线性动力学对鲁棒约束满足保证构成挑战，需一种可扩展且保留理论保证的视觉运动控制方法。

Method: (i) 利用预训练视觉特征学习低维观测映射，并附带状态相关的误差界；(ii) 设计因果仿射时变输出反馈策略，通过SLS框架优化；(iii) 提出结合序贯凸规划和高效Riccati递归的求解器，处理非凸优化问题。

Result: 在4D车辆、10D四旋翼飞行器（≥512×512像素）及59D人形机器人任务中，方法实现了安全、信息采集行为，在降低不确定性的同时保证约束满足。硬件实验验证了地面车辆的安全控制，在安全率和求解时间上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 学习驱动的视觉抽象与高效求解器的结合，使得基于SLS的安全视觉运动输出反馈控制在大规模问题中变得实用。

Abstract: We propose VISION-SLS, a method for nonlinear output-feedback control from high-resolution RGB images which provides robust constraint satisfaction guarantees under calibrated uncertainty bounds despite partial observability, sensor noise, and nonlinear dynamics. To enable scalability while retaining guarantees, we propose: (i) a learned low-dimensional observation map from pretrained visual features with state-dependent error bounds, and (ii) a causal affine time-varying output-feedback policy optimized via System Level Synthesis (SLS). We develop a scalable, novel solver for the resulting nonconvex program that leverages sequential convex programming coupled with efficient Riccati recursions. On two simulated visuomotor tasks (a 4D car and a 10D quadrotor) with >= 512 x 512 pixels and a 59D humanoid task with partial observability, our method enables safe, information-gathering behavior that reduces uncertainty while guaranteeing constraint satisfaction with empirically-calibrated error bounds. We also validate our method on hardware, safely controlling a ground vehicle from onboard images, outperforming baselines in safety rate and solve times. Together, these results show that learned visual abstractions coupled with an efficient solver make SLS-based safe visuomotor output-feedback practical at scale. The code implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/trustworthyrobotics/VISION-SLS.

</details>


### [56] [An analysis of sensor selection for fruit picking with suction-based grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24906)
*Eva Krueger,Marcus Rosette,Joseph R. Davidson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人水果采摘中难以可靠检测采摘成功状态的问题，设计并评估了一种集成于柔性吸盘式苹果夹爪的多模态感知套件，通过阶段依赖的传感器策略实现对采摘失败的前瞻性预测。实验表明随机森林和多层感知器分类器在真实果园中以超90%的准确率检测成功采摘和即将发生的失败，随机森林可在距人工标注真值0.09秒内预测采摘/滑落事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人水果采摘常因无法可靠检测水果是否成功采摘而效率低下、作物损伤率高，主要挑战包括柔性水果和夹爪、多变的茎干附着强度以及果园环境中的遮挡问题。现有研究虽探索了基于视觉感知和多传感器学习的采摘状态估计，但最小传感器集和阶段依赖的感知策略仍未被充分探索。

Method: 设计并集成了一个多模态感知套件到柔性吸盘式苹果夹爪中，采用阶段依赖的传感器评估策略，识别不同采摘阶段最具信息量的传感器组合，使用随机森林和多层感知器分类器进行采摘状态分类。

Result: 在真实苹果园实验中，随机森林和多层感知器检测成功采摘和即将发生的失败准确率超过90%，随机森林预测采摘/滑落事件的时间误差在0.09秒以内（相对于人工标注真值）。

Conclusion: 阶段依赖的多模态感知策略能够有效识别最小传感器集，在降低传感器复杂度的同时实现高精度、低延迟的采摘状态检测，为机器人采摘中的失败预测与可靠性提升提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Robotic fruit harvesting often fails to reliably detect whether a fruit has been successfully picked, limiting efficiency and increasing crop damage. This problem is difficult due to compliant fruit and grippers, variable stem attachment, and occlusions in orchard environments. Prior work has explored vision-based perception and multi-sensor learning approaches for pick state estimation. However, minimal sensor sets and phase-dependent sensing strategies for accurate pick and slip detection remain largely unexplored. In this work, we design and evaluate a multimodal sensing suite integrated into a compliant suction-based apple gripper. Our approach is unique because it identifies which sensors are most informative at different phases of the pick, enabling predictive detection of failures before they occur. The contributions of this paper are a phase-dependent evaluation of multimodal sensors and the identification of minimal sensor sets for reliable pick state classification. Experiments in a real apple orchard show that Random Forest and Multilayer Perceptron classifiers detect successful picks and impending failures with over 90% accuracy, and Random Forest predicts pick/slip events within 0.09 s of human-annotated ground truth.

</details>


### [57] [asRoBallet: Closing the Sim2Real Gap via Friction-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Underactuated Spherical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24916)
*Fang Wan,Guangyi Huang,Tianyu Wu,Zishang Zhang,Bangchao Huang,Haoran Sun,Mingdong Chen,Chaoyang Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了asRoBallet——首个成功将强化学习(RL)部署到人形球机器人硬件上的系统。通过高保真MuJoCo仿真显式建模欧姆轮离散滚子力学，并提出摩擦感知RL框架实现零样本Sim2Real迁移。同时通过减法重构利用现有四足机器人组件搭建低成本硬件平台，并开发了基于iOS生态的低延迟直觉操控系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形球机器人（ballbot）作为欠驱动和非完整控制的经典基准，存在轮-球-地面复杂摩擦模型的现实差距（reality gap）。当前LQR和MPC方法已成功实现3D平衡，但将RL迁移到实际硬件面临接触建模、执行器延迟与抖动、安全硬件探索等关键瓶颈。

Method: 1) 高保真MuJoCo仿真：显式建模ETH型全向轮的离散滚子力学，捕捉此前被忽略的寄生振动和接触不连续性；2) 摩擦感知RL框架：通过掌握轮-球和球-地界面的耦合滚动、侧向和扭转摩擦通道，实现零样本Sim2Real迁移；3) 减法重构硬件设计：从过约束四足机器人中复用关键组件，集成到新结构框架中，构建低成本稳健研究平台；4) iOS生态系统：将消费电子产品转换为低延迟接口，支持单操作员通过直觉自然运动操控复杂人形动作。

Result: 首次成功在真实人形球机器人硬件上实现了RL策略的零样本Sim2Real迁移，验证了高保真仿真和摩擦感知框架的有效性。

Conclusion: asRoBallet证明了通过精细化的接触建模和摩擦感知RL框架可以弥合仿真到现实的差距，为人形球机器人的RL部署提供了可行方案。减法重构和iOS生态系统的设计思路为低成本、易操控的机器人研究平台提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce asRoBallet, to the best of our knowledge, the first successful deployment of reinforcement learning (RL) on a humanoid ballbot hardware. Historically, ballbots have served as a canonical benchmark for underactuated and nonholonomic control, which are characterized by a reality gap in complex friction models for wheel-sphere-ground interactions. While current literature demonstrates successful handling of 3D balancing with LQR and MPC, transitioning to actual hardware for a humanoid ballbot using RL is currently hindered by critical gaps in contact modeling, actuator latency & jitter, and safe hardware exploration, and safe hardware exploration. This study proposes a high-fidelity MuJoCo simulation that explicitly models the discrete roller mechanics of ETH-type omni-wheels, thereby capturing parasitic vibrations and contact discontinuities that are previously ignored. We also developed a Friction-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework that achieves zero-shot Sim2Real transfer by mastering the coupled rolling, lateral, and torsional friction channels at the wheel-sphere and sphere-ground interfaces. We designed asRoBallet through subtractive reconfiguration, repurposing key components from an overconstrained quadruped and integrating them into a newly designed structural frame to achieve a robust research platform at low cost. We also developed a generalized iOS ecosystem that transforms consumer electronics into a low-latency interface, enabling a single operator to orchestrate expressive humanoid maneuvers via intuitive natural motion.

</details>


### [58] [Libra-VLA: Achieving Learning Equilibrium via Asynchronous Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24921)
*Yifei Wei,Linqing Zhong,Yi Liu,Yuxiang Lu,Xindong He,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Libra-VLA提出了一种粗到细的双系统VLA架构，通过将动作空间分解为离散的宏观方向意图（语义规划器）和连续的微观位姿对齐（动作精炼器），并采用异步执行策略，解决了传统VLA模型中语义-执行差距过大的问题。实验发现动作分解粒度与性能呈倒U型曲线关系，在子系统学习难度平衡时达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型采用单一扁平化生成范式，直接将视觉-语言特征映射为高频电机指令，忽略了机器人操作固有的层次性（宏观方向到达与微观位姿对齐），导致语义-执行差距过大，高层语义到连续动作的接地负担沉重。

Method: 提出粗到细双系统VLA架构：1) 语义规划器（Semantic Planner）预测离散动作token捕获宏观方向意图；2) 动作精炼器（Action Refiner）以粗意图为条件生成高频连续动作进行精确定位。采用异步执行策略实现可扩展、鲁棒、响应式的开放世界操作。

Result: 实验发现动作分解粒度与性能呈倒U型曲线关系，在两个子系统学习难度达到平衡时性能达到峰值。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦学习复杂度为粗到细的层次结构，在训练中达到平衡，同时利用结构模块化实现异步执行策略，为开放世界操作提供了可扩展、鲁棒且响应式的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation by grounding high-level semantic instructions into executable physical actions. However, prevailing approaches typically adopt a monolithic generation paradigm, directly mapping visual-linguistic features to high-frequency motor commands in a flat, non-hierarchical fashion. This strategy overlooks the inherent hierarchy of robotic manipulation, where complex actions can be naturally modeled in a Hybrid Action Space, decomposing into discrete macro-directional reaching and continuous micro-pose alignment, severely widening the semantic-actuation gap and imposing a heavy representational burden on grounding high-level semantics to continuous actions. To address this, we introduce Libra-VLA, a novel Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System VLA architecture. We explicitly decouple the learning complexity into a coarse-to-fine hierarchy to strike a training equilibrium, while simultaneously leveraging this structural modularity to implement an asynchronous execution strategy. The Semantic Planner predicts discrete action tokens capturing macro-directional intent, while the Action Refiner conditions on coarse intent to generate high-frequency continuous actions for precise alignment. Crucially, our empirical analysis reveals that performance follows an inverted-U curve relative to action decomposition granularity, peaking exactly when the learning difficulty is balanced between the two sub-systems. With the asynchronous design, our approach offers a scalable, robust, and responsive solution for open-world manipulation.

</details>


### [59] [TEACar: An Open-Source Autonomous Driving Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24934)
*Zhongzheng Zhang,Maxwell Ruyle,Andrew Kappes,Tyler Ruble,William Shaoul,Dana Moreno,Jack Penn,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TEACAR是一个1/14-1/16比例的模块化自动驾驶平台，采用四层甲板结构解耦感知、计算、执行和电源子系统，基于ROS 2软件架构，通过CNN转向控制器验证了其作为ITS研究、教育和开发可扩展测试平台的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小型自主赛车平台存在模块化程度有限、集成复杂度高、可扩展性受限等问题，无法有效支持ITS中基于视觉感知和学习控制的硬件在环验证需求。

Method: 设计四层甲板机械架构（感知、计算、执行、电源物理解耦），采用硬件抽象和ROS 2软件架构，构建1/14-1/16比例原型系统，并通过三种CNN转向控制器进行综合评估。

Result: 量化了机械稳定性、结构特性和软件性能；测量了推理延迟、功耗和系统运行时间，验证了计算能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TEACAR为ITS研究、教育和开发提供了一个可扩展、模块化且成本效益高的测试平台。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) increasingly rely on vision-based perception and learning-based control, necessitating experimental platforms that support realistic hardware-in-the-loop validation. Small-scale platforms for autonomous racing offer a practical path to hardware validation, but often suffer from limited modularity, high integration complexity, or restricted extensibility. This paper presents TEACAR, a 1/14- to 1/16-scale autonomous driving platform designed with modular mechanical architecture, hardware abstraction, and ROS 2-based software. The system adopts a four-layer deck structure that physically decouples sensing, computation, actuation, and power subsystems, improving structural rigidity while simplifying reconfiguration. We constructed and comprehensively evaluated the prototype of TEACAR. Its mechanical stability, structural characteristics, and software performance were quantified based on three CNN-based steering controllers. Inference latency, power consumption, and system operating time were measured to evaluate computational capability and robustness. Our experiments demonstrated that TEACAR offers a scalable, modular, and cost-effective testbed for ITS research, education, and development. Our project repository is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [60] [DiscreteRTC: Discrete Diffusion Policies are Natural Asynchronous Executors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25050)
*Pengcheng Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Chensheng Peng,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Chenfeng Xu,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DiscreteRTC，利用离散扩散策略的原生掩码修复能力实现异步执行，无需额外微调，在动态物理任务中比连续RTC更快、更简单、成功率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI必须在动态环境中边思考边行动，同步执行器的帧间停顿对动态任务是致命的。现有基于流匹配的实时分块（RTC）存在结构缺陷：其修复（inpainting）来自推理时的外部修正而非基础策略，导致预训练收益低、需要特定微调、依赖启发式引导且增加延迟开销。

Method: 观察到离散扩散策略（通过迭代去掩码生成动作）天然具备异步执行能力：修复是其原生操作，无需微调；早停机制可提供自适应引导并降低推理成本。据此提出DiscreteRTC，用原生去掩码替代外部修正。

Result: 在动态仿真基准和真实世界动态操作任务上，DiscreteRTC成功率高于连续RTC及其他基线。异步修复零代码实现，推理计算量仅为从头生成动作的0.7倍，在真实世界动态抓取任务中成功率比基于流匹配的RTC高50%。

Conclusion: DiscreteRTC更简单（无需额外异步修复代码）、更快（推理计算量减少）、更好（成功率显著提升），是物理AI异步执行的有效方案。

Abstract: Unlike chatbots, physical AI must act while the world keeps evolving. Therefore, the inter-chunk pause of synchronous executors are fatal for dynamic tasks regardless of how fast the inference is. Asynchronous execution -- thinking while acting -- is therefore a structural requirement, and real-time chunking (RTC) makes it viable by recasting chunk transitions as inpainting: freezing committed actions and consistently generating the remainder. However, RTC with flow-matching policy is structurally suboptimal: its inpainting comes from inference-time corrections rather than the base policy, yielding little pre-training benefit, specific fine-tuning, heuristic guidance, and extra computation that inflates the latency. In this work, we observe that discrete diffusion policies, which generate actions by iteratively unmasking, are natural asynchronous executors that resolve all limitations at once: they are fine-tuning free since inpainting is their native operation, while early stopping further provides adaptive guidance and reduces inference cost. We propose DiscreteRTC, which replaces external corrections with native unmasking, and show on dynamic simulated benchmarks and real-world dynamic manipulation tasks that it achieves higher success rates than continuous RTC and other baselines. In summary, DiscreteRTC is simpler to implement with 0 lines of code for async inpainting, faster at inference with only 0.7x computation compared with generating actions from scratch, and better at execution with 50% higher success rate in real-world dynamic pick task compared with flow-matching-based RTC. More visualizations are on https://outsider86.github.io/DiscreteRTCSite/.

</details>


### [61] [HANDFUL: Sequential Grasp-Conditioned Dexterous Manipulation with Resource Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25126)
*Ethan Foong,Yunshuang Li,Hao Jiang,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HANDFUL框架，通过资源感知抓取（保留手指用于后续任务）和课程学习，解决灵巧手在多技能序列操作中的抓取条件操控问题，实验证明该方法优于贪婪优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手操作研究多聚焦于单物体单技能任务，而实际多技能序列操作中，机器人需在执行后续子任务时保持对已抓取物体的控制，因此需要能保留手指资源供后续动作使用的抓取策略。

Method: 提出HANDFUL框架：(1) 将手指使用建模为有限资源，通过手指级接触奖励鼓励探索资源感知抓取；(2) 采用基于课程学习的策略选择抓取以适配下游任务；(3) 构建HANDFUL-Bench仿真基准，包含推、拉、按等第二子任务。

Result: 仿真实验表明，优先考虑资源感知抓取相比贪婪优化初始抓取的基线方法，在第二子任务成功率和鲁棒性上均有提升；并在真实LEAP灵巧手上进行了验证。

Conclusion: 建立了资源感知抓取规划作为多功能灵巧操作的关键原则，为序列化灵巧操作提供了有效的学习框架和基准。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands offer rich opportunities for multifunctional manipulation, where a robot must execute multiple skills in sequence while maintaining control over previously grasped objects. Most prior work in dexterous manipulation focuses on single-object, single-skill tasks. In contrast, our insight is that many sequential tasks require resource-aware grasps that conserve fingers for future actions. In this paper, we study sequential grasp-conditioned dexterous manipulation, where a robot first grasps an object and then performs a second, distinct manipulation subtask while preserving the initial grasp. We introduce HANDFUL, a learning framework that models finger usage as a limited resource and encourages exploration of resource-aware grasps through finger-level contact rewards. These grasps are subsequently selected for downstream tasks via curriculum-based policy learning. We further propose HANDFUL-Bench, a simulation benchmark that introduces sequential dexterous manipulation tasks across multiple secondsubtask objectives, including pushing, pulling, and pressing, under a shared grasp-conditioned setup. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that prioritizing resource-aware grasps improves second-subtask success and robustness compared to a baseline that greedily optimizes the initial grasp before attempting the second subtask. We additionally validate our approach on a real dexterous LEAP hand. Together, this work establishes resource-aware grasp planning as a key principle for multifunctional dexterous manipulation. Supplementary material is available on our website: https://handful-dex.github.io.

</details>


### [62] [Slot-hopping Enabled Loiter Guidance and Automation for Fixed-wing UAV Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25292)
*Pradeep J,Siddhardha Kedarisetty,Ashwini Ratnoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对固定翼无人机走廊的交通拥堵管理问题，进一步发展了近期提出的loiter-lane框架，提出了一种半协同引导策略，使进入的无人机在速度约束下先尝试与现有空位会合，若不可行则通过最少悬停无人机协调跳槽创建空位，以最小干扰实现紧凑编队插入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决固定翼无人机走廊中的交通拥堵管理问题，在最小化对已有无人机干扰的同时，实现更紧凑的固定翼无人机走廊编队插入。

Method: 提出半协同引导策略：进入无人机首先在速度约束内尝试与现有空位会合；若直接插入不可行，则协调最少数量悬停无人机执行跳槽操作以创建合适空位。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了该方法的可行性和性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持紧凑编队的同时，能有效减少对已有无人机的干扰，为固定翼无人机走廊的交通管理提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of traffic congestion management in fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) corridors by further developing a recently introduced loiter-lane framework. A semi-cooperative guidance strategy is developed for inserting fixed-wing UAVs into a loiter lane with minimal disruption to the UAVs already operating within it, while enabling a more compact fixed-wing UAV corridor. Building on the concepts of cooperative and non-disruptive loiter-lane insertion, the proposed strategy makes the incoming UAV first attempt, within its speed bounds, to rendezvous with an existing empty loiter slot. If direct insertion is infeasible, a minimal number of loitering UAVs perform coordinated slot hopping to create a suitably positioned empty slot. The feasibility and performance of the method are demonstrated through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [63] [ANCHOR: A Physically Grounded Closed-Loop Framework for Robust Home-Service Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25323)
*Jinhao Jiang,Shengyu Fang,Sibo Zuo,Yujie Tang,Yirui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ANCHOR框架，通过物理锚定的闭环执行机制解决移动操作机器人在开放环境中符号计划与物理世界不一致的问题，在60次真实机器人实验中任务成功率从53.3%提升至71.7%，扰动恢复率达71.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇移动操作系统中存在三类执行不一致问题：依赖预扫描语义地图导致场景变化后不一致；导航终点未考虑下游操作可行性，导致"到达但不可操作"；异常处理采用无差别的全局重规划，无法有效隔离局部错误。这些问题源于符号计划与演化的物理世界之间的脱节。

Method: ANCHOR框架集成三种机制：(1)物理锚定任务规划——将符号谓词绑定到可观测几何锚点并在每个动作后重新验证；(2)可操作性感知基座对齐——确保导航终点满足运动学可达性和局部碰撞可行性；(3)最小责任层分层恢复——在感知、基座-手臂协调和执行层定位故障，防止级联重试。

Result: 在60次未见过的真实环境机器人试验中，任务成功率从53.3%提升至71.7%，扰动下恢复率达71.4%。

Conclusion: 显式的物理锚定和结构化的故障隔离对于鲁棒的移动操作至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in open-vocabulary mobile manipulation have brought robots into real domestic environments. In such settings, reliable long-horizon execution under open-set object references and frequent disturbances becomes essential. However, many failures persist. These are not caused by semantic misunderstanding but by inconsistencies between symbolic plans and the evolving physical world, manifested as three recurring limitations: (i) existing systems often rely on pre-scanned semantic maps that become inconsistent after scene changes and disturbances; (ii) they select navigation endpoints without considering downstream manipulation feasibility, causing the "arrived but inoperable" problem; and (iii) they handle anomalies through undifferentiated global replanning, which often fails to contain local errors. To address this execution inconsistency, we present ANCHOR, a physically grounded closed-loop framework that aligns symbolic reasoning with verifiable physical state during execution. ANCHOR integrates three mechanisms: (i) physically anchored task planning, which binds symbolic predicates to observable geometric anchors and re-validates them after each action; (ii) operability-aware base alignment, which ensures that navigation endpoints satisfy kinematic reachability and local collision feasibility; and (iii) minimum-responsible-layer hierarchical recovery, which localizes failures across perception, base-arm coordination, and execution layers to prevent cascading retries. Across 60 real-robot trials in previously unseen environments, ANCHOR improves task success from 53.3% to 71.7% and achieves a 71.4% recovery rate under perturbations, demonstrating that explicit physical grounding and structured failure containment are critical for robust mobile manipulation. Our project page is available at https://anchor9178.github.io/ANCHOR/ .

</details>


### [64] [ProDrive: Proactive Planning for Autonomous Driving via Ego-Environment Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25329)
*Chuyao Fu,Shengzhe Gan,Zhuoli Ouyang,Yuhan Rui,Xiaowei Chi,Sirui Han,Jiankun Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProDrive提出了一种基于世界模型的前瞻性规划框架，通过联合训练查询中心轨迹规划器和BEV世界模型，实现自车-环境协同演化，在NAVSIM v1数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶规划器仅依赖当前观测生成轨迹，缺乏对未来场景演化的预测能力，导致短视决策和安全隐患。

Method: 联合训练查询中心轨迹规划器（生成多样化候选轨迹和规划感知的自车token）与BEV世界模型（基于规划条件预测未来场景演化），通过双向耦合保持端到端梯度流动，实现并行评估所有候选轨迹。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上，ProDrive在安全性和规划效率方面均优于强基线，消融实验验证了自车-环境耦合设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 双向耦合的世界模型-规划器框架能够实现超越当前观测驱动的主动规划，为自动驾驶提供更安全高效的决策能力。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners typically generate trajectories from current observations alone. However, real-world driving is highly dynamic, and such reactive planning cannot anticipate future scene evolution, often leading to myopic decisions and safety-critical failures. We propose ProDrive, a world-model-based proactive planning framework that enables ego-environment co-evolution for autonomous driving. ProDrive jointly trains a query-centric trajectory planner and a bird's-eye-view (BEV) world model end-to-end: the planner generates diverse candidate trajectories and planning-aware ego tokens, while the world model predicts future scene evolution conditioned on them. By injecting planner features into the world model and evaluating all candidates in parallel, ProDrive preserves end-to-end gradient flow and allows future outcome assessment to directly shape planning. This bidirectional coupling enables proactive planning beyond current-observation-driven decision-making. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 show that ProDrive outperforms strong baselines in both safety and planning efficiency, while ablations validate the effectiveness of the proposed ego-environment coupling design.

</details>


### [65] [Robust Graph Matching through Semantic Relationship Generation for SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25404)
*David Perez-Saura,Jose Andres Millan-Romera,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Holger Voos,Pascual Campoy,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义增强的图匹配方法，通过显式建模检测对象与结构元素（房间、墙平面）之间的关系，在重复或对称布局环境中有效减少歧义，提升图匹配的效率和鲁棒性，并在iS-Graphs框架中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化室内环境中，基于场景图的定位方法在面对重复或对称布局时，仅依赖结构线索往往不足以解决匹配歧义，导致定位失败或效率低下。

Method: 从RGB-D数据中检测对象并将其集成到图中，显式建模对象与房间、墙平面等结构元素之间的语义关系，利用这些关系在几何验证之前过滤候选对应关系，从而减少歧义和搜索复杂度。该方法集成在iS-Graphs框架中。

Result: 在合成和模拟环境中的实验表明，语义关系显著减少了候选匹配数量，提升了计算效率，并实现了更快的收敛，特别是在纯几何方法失效的对称场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 语义增强的图匹配方法能够有效解决对称/重复布局环境下的定位歧义问题，在减少计算开销的同时提升匹配准确性，是对纯几何方法的有效补充。

Abstract: Graph-based representations such as Scene Graphs enable localization in structured indoor environments by matching a locally observed graph, constructed from sensor data, to a prior map. This process is particularly challenging in environments with repetitive or symmetric layouts, where structural cues alone are often insufficient to resolve ambiguities. We propose a semantic-enhanced graph matching approach that explicitly models relations between detected objects and structural elements, such as rooms and wall planes. Objects are detected from RGB-D data and integrated into the graph, and their relations to structural elements are exploited to filter candidate correspondences prior to geometric verification, significantly reducing ambiguity and search complexity. The proposed method is integrated within the iS-Graphs framework and evaluated in synthetic and simulated environments. Results show that semantic relations significantly reduce the number of candidate matches, improve computational efficiency, and enable faster convergence, particularly in symmetric scenarios where purely geometric approaches fail.

</details>


### [66] [GS-Playground: A High-Throughput Photorealistic Simulator for Vision-Informed Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25459)
*Yufei Jia,Heng Zhang,Ziheng Zhang,Junzhe Wu,Mingrui Yu,Zifan Wang,Dixuan Jiang,Zheng Li,Chenyu Cao,Zhuoyuan Yu,Xun Yang,Haizhou Ge,Yuchi Zhang,Jiayuan Zhang,Zhenbiao Huang,Tianle Liu,Shenyu Chen,Jiacheng Wang,Bin Xie,Xuran Yao,Xiwa Deng,Guangyu Wang,Jinzhi Zhang,Lei Hao,Zhixing Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Anqi Wang,Hongyun Tian,Yiyi Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yizhou Jiang,Hanyang Shao,Yue Li,Lu Shi,Bokui Chen,Wei Sui,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GS-Playground是一个多模态仿真框架，通过集成批量3D高斯泼溅渲染管道与高性能并行物理引擎，实现了10^4 FPS（640x480分辨率）的视觉渲染吞吐量，并引入自动化Real2Sim工作流，有效加速了具身AI中的端到端感知学习，在 locomotion、导航和操作任务上弥合了感知与物理域间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模并行模拟器在基于本体感觉的运动任务上取得了突破，但在视觉信息任务中因大规模逼真渲染的计算开销过高而潜力受限；同时，仿真就绪的3D资产创建依赖繁琐的手工建模，且仿真到现实的物理差距阻碍了接触式操作策略的迁移。

Method: 提出GS-Playground框架：1）开发高性能并行物理引擎，与批量3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）渲染管道集成，实现高保真同步；2）设计自动化Real2Sim工作流，重建照片级逼真、物理一致且内存高效的仿真环境。

Result: 系统在640x480分辨率下达到10^4 FPS的吞吐量，显著降低大规模视觉强化学习门槛；在运动、导航和操作任务上的大量实验表明，GS-Playground有效弥合了不同具身任务中的感知和物理差距。

Conclusion: GS-Playground通过高效的视觉渲染与物理仿真集成，为大规模视觉RL提供了可行方案，自动化场景重建流程进一步降低了仿真环境构建的成本，有望推动视觉中心具身AI的研究。

Abstract: Embodied AI research is undergoing a shift toward vision-centric perceptual paradigms. While massively parallel simulators have catalyzed breakthroughs in proprioception-based locomotion, their potential remains largely untapped for vision-informed tasks due to the prohibitive computational overhead of large-scale photorealistic rendering. Furthermore, the creation of simulation-ready 3D assets heavily relies on labor-intensive manual modeling, while the significant sim-to-real physical gap hinders the transfer of contact-rich manipulation policies. To address these bottlenecks, we propose GS-Playground, a multi-modal simulation framework designed to accelerate end-to-end perceptual learning. We develop a novel high-performance parallel physics engine, specifically designed to integrate with a batch 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) rendering pipeline to ensure high-fidelity synchronization. Our system achieves a breakthrough throughput of 10^4 FPS at 640x480 resolution, significantly lowering the barrier for large-scale visual RL. Additionally, we introduce an automated Real2Sim workflow that reconstructs photorealistic, physically consistent, and memory-efficient environments, streamlining the generation of complex simulation-ready scenes. Extensive experiments on locomotion, navigation, and manipulation demonstrate that GS-Playground effectively bridges the perceptual and physical gaps across diverse embodied tasks. Project homepage: https://gsplayground.github.io.

</details>


### [67] [Egocentric Tactile and Proximity Sensors as Observation Priors for Humanoid Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25554)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Niraj Pudasaini,William Xie,Naren Sivagnanadasan,Nikolaus Correll,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种强化学习框架，用于人形机器人H1-2的全身避碰，并在躲避球任务中研究了传感器特性（覆盖范围、类型、距离）对学习行为的影响，发现原始近距离测量可替代显式物体定位，且稀疏非定向信号比密集定向信号更具样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管触觉和近距传感器因其抗遮挡特性而被用于机器人避碰，但如何设计传感器特性（覆盖范围、类型、距离）以实现有效的避让行为尚不明确。

Method: 在H1-2人形机器人上构建了基于强化学习的全身避碰框架，以躲避球为基准任务，消融研究了分布在上半身的传感器属性（覆盖范围、类型、距离）对学习行为的影响。

Result: 原始近距测量可以在足够传感距离下替代显式物体定位；稀疏非定向近距信号在样本效率上优于密集定向信号。

Conclusion: 传感器特性显著影响强化学习避碰行为的表现，合理设计传感器类型和范围可提升学习效率和避碰效果。

Abstract: Collision-free motion is often aided by tactile and proximity sensors distributed on the body of the robot due to their resistance to occlusion as opposed to external cameras. However, how to shape the sensor's properties, such as sensing coverage; type; and range, to enable avoidant behavior remains unclear. In this work, we present a reinforcement learning framework for whole-body collision avoidance on a humanoid H1-2 robot and use it to characterize how sensor properties shape learned avoidance behavior. Using dodgeball as a benchmark task, we ablate the properties of sensors distributed across the upper body of the robot and find that raw proximity measurements can substitute for explicit object localization provided the sensing range is sufficient and that sparse non-directional proximity signals outpace dense directional alternatives in sample efficiency.

</details>


### [68] [Improving Sensing Coverage and Compliance of 3D-Printed Artificial Skins Through Multi-Modal Sensing and Soft Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25563)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Caleb Escobedo,Sayak Ray,Alexander Dickhans,Anna Soukhovei,Nickolaus Jackoski,Lyle Antieau,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种3D打印混合飞行时间（ToF）和自电容（SC）传感人造皮肤，实现了多模态感知集成、软质顺应性覆盖及简化电气接口，并在FR3机器人手臂上部署6个单元共40个传感元件，展示了接触检测、场景重建和压力相关触觉响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的3D打印人造皮肤局限于单模态传感和刚性材料，实用性和功能集成度不足。本文旨在通过多模态传感集成、软质顺应性覆盖和简化电气接口来提升3D打印人造皮肤的实际可用性。

Method: 采用混合ToF和SC传感机制，结合软质顺应性覆盖层实现冲击吸收和压力传感；通过优化打印导电迹线与外部电子器件的电气接口实现流线化连接；在FR3机器人手臂上部署6个单元共40个传感元件进行验证。

Result: 成功实现了多模态传感功能，包括接触检测、场景重建以及带有顺应性覆盖层的压力相关触觉响应。

Conclusion: 混合ToF和SC传感的3D打印人造皮肤具备多模态感知集成能力，结合软质覆盖层可提供更丰富的触觉信息，提升了3D打印人造皮肤在实际机器人应用中的实用性和功能性。

Abstract: 3D-printed artificial skins are a scalable approach to whole-body tactile and proximity coverage, but prior implementations have been limited to unimodal sensing and rigid materials. To improve the practical usability of 3D-printed artificial skins, we present a hybrid time-of-flight (ToF) and self-capacitance (SC) sensing skin that demonstrates multi-modal sensing integration, soft compliant coverings for impact absorption and pressure sensing, and a streamlined electrical interface between printed conductive traces and external electronics. We show that combining ToF and SC modalities enables contact detection, scene reconstruction, and pressure-correlated tactile responses with the compliant covering by deploying six artificial skin units with 40 sensing elements over an FR3 robot arm.

</details>


### [69] [SlicerRoboTMS: An Open-Source 3D Slicer Extension for Robot-Assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25661)
*Wenzhi Bai,Yituo Guo,Bhaskar Basu,Andrew Weightman,Zhenhong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SlicerRoboTMS，一个基于3D Slicer的开源扩展，为机器人辅助经颅磁刺激（Robo-TMS）研究提供统一的交互基础设施，支持MRI引导的神经导航和机器人系统集成，降低了该跨学科领域的研究门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助经颅磁刺激（Robo-TMS）的开发需要医学影像、计算机视觉和机器人学等多学科专业知识，技术门槛高，缺乏统一的、可扩展的开源研究平台。

Method: 基于3D Slicer开发开源扩展SlicerRoboTMS，利用其医学图像计算和可视化能力，通过标准化通信协议和可配置系统描述实现MRI神经导航与机器人系统的接口集成。

Result: 提供了一个示例集成流程，展示了SlicerRoboTMS在代表性Robo-TMS工作流中的应用。扩展代码已开源在GitHub上。

Conclusion: SlicerRoboTMS通过支持多样化硬件配置和快速原型开发，降低了Robo-TMS研究的准入门槛，促进了该领域的可重复和可扩展研究。

Abstract: Robot-assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (Robo-TMS) is an image-guided robotic intervention that enhances the accuracy and reproducibility of conventional Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (TMS), a widely used non-invasive brain stimulation procedure in clinical treatment and neuroscience research. Despite its potential, the development of Robo-TMS remains challenging due to the need for multidisciplinary expertise spanning medical imaging, computer vision, and robotics. This paper presents SlicerRoboTMS, an open-source 3D Slicer extension that provides a unified interaction infrastructure for Robo-TMS research. By leveraging 3D Slicer's medical image computing and visualisation capabilities, the extension supports Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI)-based neuronavigation and interfaces with robotic systems through standardised communication protocols and configurable system descriptions. An example integration is presented to demonstrate how SlicerRoboTMS can be incorporated into a representative Robo-TMS workflow. Designed to support diverse hardware configurations and rapid prototyping, SlicerRoboTMS lowers the barrier to entry and facilitates reproducible and extensible research in Robo-TMS. The extension is available at https://github.com/OpenRoboTMS/SlicerRoboTMS.

</details>


### [70] [GEGLU-Transformer for IMU-to-EMG Estimation with Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25670)
*Miroljub Mihailovic,Luca Tonin,Stefano Tortora,Emanuele Menegatti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer+GEGLU的自适应IMU-to-EMG学习框架，通过惯性测量单元信号重建肌肉激活包络，在跨受试者泛化和快速个性化方面表现出色，仅用0.5%的适应数据即可达到与直接EMG传感相媲美的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面EMG在实验室外难以鲁棒部署，存在电极敏感性、信号非平稳性和强受试者依赖性等问题，亟需一种实用且可扩展的替代方案用于可穿戴机器人的自适应控制。

Method: 采用Transformer编码器结合高斯误差门控线性单元（GEGLU-Transformer），从可穿戴惯性测量数据中连续重建肌肉激活包络；通过轻量级受试者特定适应实现快速个性化。

Result: 严格LOSO协议下，无适应时r=0.706±0.139，R²=0.474±0.208；仅用0.5%适应数据后，性能提升至r=0.761±0.030，R²=0.559±0.047，且早期性能即趋于饱和。

Conclusion: 注意力机制架构结合轻量级适应可作为直接EMG传感的实用且可扩展替代方案，适用于现实世界的可穿戴机器人应用。

Abstract: Reliable estimation of neuromuscular activation is a key enabler for adaptive and personalized control in wearable robotics. However, surface electromyography (EMG) remains difficult to deploy robustly outside laboratory settings due to electrode sensitivity, signal non-stationarity, and strong subject dependence. In this work, we propose an adaptive IMU-to-EMG learning framework that reconstructs continuous muscle activation envelopes from wearable inertial measurements across heterogeneous movement conditions. The approach combines a Transformer encoder with Gaussian Error Gated Linear Units (GEGLU-Transformer) to enhance cross-subject generalization and enable rapid subject-specific personalization. Under a strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocol on a multi-condition lower-limb biomechanics dataset, the proposed architecture achieves r = 0.706 +/- 0.139 and R^2 = 0.474 +/- 0.208 without subject-specific adaptation. With only 0.5% adaptation data, performance increases to r = 0.761 +/- 0.030 and R^2 = 0.559 +/- 0.047, demonstrating rapid adaptation and early performance saturation. These results support attention-based architectures combined with lightweight adaptation as a practical and scalable alternative to direct EMG sensing for real-world wearable robotic applications.

</details>


### [71] [Learning-Based Dynamics Modeling and Robust Control for Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25691)
*Ziqing Zou,Ke Qiu,Fei Wang,Haojian Lu,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微学习框架，结合GRU动力学模型（双向多通道连接+残差预测）与端到端神经控制策略，通过反向传播优化实现对绳驱连续体机器人的高精度跟踪和对未知负载的鲁棒控制，消除了雅可比方法中的自激振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳驱连续体机器人因摩擦迟滞和传动柔顺等复杂非线性，导致建模和控制极具挑战，现有方法难以应对。

Method: 1) 构建基于GRU的动力学模型，采用双向多通道连接和残差预测以抑制长时自回归误差累积；2) 将该模型作为梯度桥接，通过反向传播优化端到端神经控制策略，隐式内化非线性补偿。

Result: 在三段式TDCR实物平台上实现了精确跟踪，对未知负载表现出优越鲁棒性，消除了雅可比方法中的自激振荡。

Conclusion: 所提可微学习框架有效解决了TDCR的建模与控制难题，在跟踪精度和鲁棒性上显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots (TDCRs) pose significant modeling and control challenges due to complex nonlinearities, such as frictional hysteresis and transmission compliance. This paper proposes a differentiable learning framework that integrates high-fidelity dynamics modeling with robust neural control. We develop a GRU-based dynamics model featuring bidirectional multi-channel connectivity and residual prediction to effectively suppress compounding errors during long-horizon auto-regressive prediction. By treating this model as a gradient bridge, an end-to-end neural control policy is optimized through backpropagation, allowing it to implicitly internalize compensation for intricate nonlinearities. Experimental validation on a physical three-section TDCR demonstrates that our framework achieves accurate tracking and superior robustness against unseen payloads, outperforming Jacobian-based methods by eliminating self-excited oscillations.

</details>


### [72] [Sensitivity-Based Tube NMPC for Cooperative Aerial Structures Under Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25766)
*Giuseppe Silano,Quentin Sablé,Marco Tognon,Luigi Iannelli,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于灵敏度的管式非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架，用于有界参数不确定性下的协作式空中链。针对由刚性连杆连接的平面两车链，在考虑输入速率驱动（限制推力和扭矩的摆率和幅度）的模型下，通过沿时域传播一阶参数状态灵敏度并在线计算约束收紧裕度，实现了对连杆质量、长度和惯性的鲁棒性。利用平滑余弦嵌入的鲁棒化链间分离约束和推力幅值边界，在MATLAB中实现并通过边界贴近机动和蒙特卡洛不确定性采样评估。结果表明，在不确定性下约束裕度得到改善，跟踪性能与标称NMPC相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的空中协作链系统（如多旋翼拖拽负载）面临连杆参数（质量、长度、惯性）的不确定性，传统标称NMPC无法保证约束满足，需要鲁棒控制方法确保安全运行。

Method: 采用基于灵敏度分析的管式NMPC：沿预测时域传播一阶参数状态灵敏度，在线计算约束收紧裕度；通过平滑余弦嵌入实现鲁棒化链间分离约束；结合输入速率驱动模型处理推力和扭矩的摆率与幅度限制。

Result: 在边界贴近机动和蒙特卡洛不确定性采样测试中，该方法在保持与标称NMPC相当的跟踪性能下，显著改善了不确定性下的约束满足裕度。

Conclusion: 基于灵敏度的管式NMPC能够有效处理空中协作链的参数不确定性，在不牺牲跟踪性能的前提下增强鲁棒性，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a sensitivity-based tube Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) framework for cooperative aerial chains under bounded parametric uncertainty. We consider a planar two-vehicle chain connected by rigid links, modeled with input-rate actuation to enforce slew-rate and magnitude limits on thrust and torque. Robustness to uncertainty in link mass, length, and inertia is achieved by propagating first-order parametric state sensitivities along the horizon and using them to compute online constraint-tightening margins. We robustify an inter-link separation constraint, implemented via a smooth cosine embedding, and thrust-magnitude bounds. The method is implemented in MATLAB and evaluated with boundary-hugging maneuvers and Monte-Carlo uncertainty sampling. Results show improved constraint margins under uncertainty with tracking performance comparable to nominal NMPC.

</details>


### [73] [KinDER: A Physical Reasoning Benchmark for Robot Learning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25788)
*Yixuan Huang,Bowen Li,Vaibhav Saxena,Yichao Liang,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Liang Ji,Lihan Zha,Jimmy Wu,Nishanth Kumar,Sebastian Scherer,Danfei Xu,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KinDER是一个面向机器人运动学与动力学物理推理的基准测试平台，包含25个程序化生成环境、参数化技能库和13种基线方法，实证表明现有方法在物理推理方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统在与物理世界交互时需推理自身具身、环境和任务带来的运动学与动力学约束，但现有方法在物理推理方面存在明显缺陷，亟需一个标准化的基准来系统评估和比较不同方法。

Method: 设计25个程序化生成环境，每个环境针对五种核心物理推理挑战之一（基本空间关系、非夹取多物体操作、工具使用、组合几何约束、动态约束），构建Gymnasium兼容的Python库，提供参数化技能和演示，实现13种基线方法涵盖任务与运动规划、模仿学习、强化学习和基础模型方法，并包含真实-仿真-真实实验验证。

Result: 现有方法（包括任务与运动规划、模仿学习、强化学习和基础模型方法）在多个环境中表现不佳，难以解决许多物理推理任务，揭示了当前方法在物理推理方面的显著差距。

Conclusion: KinDER为机器人物理推理提供了系统比较的平台，揭示了现有方法的不足，全开源以推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Robotic systems that interact with the physical world must reason about kinematic and dynamic constraints imposed by their own embodiment, their environment, and the task at hand. We introduce KinDER, a benchmark for Kinematic and Dynamic Embodied Reasoning that targets physical reasoning challenges arising in robot learning and planning. KinDER comprises 25 procedurally generated environments, a Gymnasium-compatible Python library with parameterized skills and demonstrations, and a standardized evaluation suite with 13 implemented baselines spanning task and motion planning, imitation learning, reinforcement learning, and foundation-model-based approaches. The environments are designed to isolate five core physical reasoning challenges: basic spatial relations, nonprehensile multi-object manipulation, tool use, combinatorial geometric constraints, and dynamic constraints, disentangled from perception, language understanding, and application-specific complexity. Empirical evaluation shows that existing methods struggle to solve many of the environments, indicating substantial gaps in current approaches to physical reasoning. We additionally include real-to-sim-to-real experiments on a mobile manipulator to assess the correspondence between simulation and real-world physical interaction. KinDER is fully open-sourced and intended to enable systematic comparison across diverse paradigms for advancing physical reasoning in robotics. Website and code: https://prpl-group.com/kinder-site/

</details>

### [74] [Co-Director: Agentic Generative Video Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24842)
*Yale Song,Yiwen Song,Nick Losier,Nathan Hodson,Ye Jin,Rhyard Zhu,Yan Xu,Daniel Vlasic,Carina Claassen,Jasmine Leon,Khanh G. LeViet,Zack Chomyn,Joe Timmons,Brett Slatkin,Scott Penberthy,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Co-Director是一个层次化多智能体框架，将视频故事生成形式化为全局优化问题，通过多臂老虎机进行全局创意方向探索和局部多模态自优化循环确保语义连贯性，在GenAD-Bench数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型虽能生成高质量视频片段，但将其转化为连贯故事引擎仍具挑战。当前agentic流水线通过链式模块自动化这一过程，但由于独立的手工提示设计，存在语义漂移和级联故障问题。

Method: 提出Co-Director层次化多智能体框架：(1) 将视频故事生成建模为全局优化问题；(2) 引入层次化参数化——多臂老虎机全局识别有前景的创意方向，局部多模态自优化循环缓解身份漂移并确保序列级一致性；(3) 平衡新颖叙事策略的探索与有效创意配置的利用。

Result: 在包含400个场景的虚构产品个性化广告数据集GenAD-Bench上，Co-Director显著优于当前最先进的基线方法，并能无缝泛化到更广泛的电影叙事场景。

Conclusion: Co-Director提供了一种 principled 的方法，有效解决了视频故事生成中的语义连贯性问题，超越了现有agentic流水线方法，具有广泛的泛化能力。

Abstract: While diffusion models generate high-fidelity video clips, transforming them into coherent storytelling engines remains challenging. Current agentic pipelines automate this via chained modules but suffer from semantic drift and cascading failures due to independent, handcrafted prompting. We present Co-Director, a hierarchical multi-agent framework formalizing video storytelling as a global optimization problem. To ensure semantic coherence, we introduce hierarchical parameterization: a multi-armed bandit globally identifies promising creative directions, while a local multimodal self-refinement loop mitigates identity drift and ensures sequence-level consistency. This balances the exploration of novel narrative strategies with the exploitation of effective creative configurations. For evaluation, we introduce GenAD-Bench, a 400-scenario dataset of fictional products for personalized advertising. Experiments demonstrate that Co-Director significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, offering a principled approach that seamlessly generalizes to broader cinematic narratives. Project Page: https://co-director-agent.github.io/

</details>


### [75] [Latent Agents: A Post-Training Procedure for Internalized Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24881)
*John Seon Keun Yi,Aaron Mueller,Dokyun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将多智能体辩论蒸馏到单一LLM的两阶段微调框架，在匹配或超越显式多智能体辩论性能的同时，最多可减少93%的token消耗。通过激活引导研究，发现蒸馏过程在激活空间中创建了对应不同智能体视角的可解释子空间，并展示了通过注入有害智能体再进行负向引导，可以更容易地定位和控制有害行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论虽能提升LLM推理能力，但需要生成长篇辩论记录，计算成本高昂。现有方法效率低下，亟需一种更高效的方式在不牺牲性能的前提下获得多智能体辩论的优势。

Method: 提出两阶段微调框架：第一阶段进行辩论结构学习（debate structure learning），第二阶段通过动态奖励调度（dynamic reward scheduling）和长度裁剪（length clipping）进行内化（internalization）。随后采用激活引导（activation steering）探究其机制基础。

Result: 内化模型在多个模型和基准上匹配或超越显式多智能体辩论性能，同时最多减少93%的token使用。激活引导分析揭示了蒸馏过程中形成了对应不同智能体视角的可解释激活子空间。通过注入有害智能体再进行负向引导，有害行为更容易被定位和控制，且对通用性能的损害更小。

Conclusion: 多智能体辩论能力可以通过蒸馏有效内化到单一模型中，且内化后的模型激活空间中存在可解释的智能体特定子空间，这为理解和控制内化推理行为提供了新视角和实用指南。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate has been shown to improve reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, it is compute-intensive, requiring generation of long transcripts before answering questions. To address this inefficiency, we develop a framework that distills multi-agent debate into a single LLM through a two-stage fine-tuning pipeline combining debate structure learning with internalization via dynamic reward scheduling and length clipping. Across multiple models and benchmarks, our internalized models match or exceed explicit multi-agent debate performance using up to 93% fewer tokens. We then investigate the mechanistic basis of this capability through activation steering, finding that internalization creates agent-specific subspaces: interpretable directions in activation space corresponding to different agent perspectives. We further demonstrate a practical application: by instilling malicious agents into the LLM through internalized debate, then applying negative steering to suppress them, we show that distillation makes harmful behaviors easier to localize and control with smaller reductions in general performance compared to steering base models. Our findings offer a new perspective for understanding multi-agent capabilities in distilled models and provide practical guidelines for controlling internalized reasoning behaviors. Code available at https://github.com/johnsk95/latent_agents

</details>


### [76] [S-SONDO: Self-Supervised Knowledge Distillation for General Audio Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24933)
*Mohammed Ali El Adlouni,Aurian Quelennec,Pierre Chouteau,Geoffroy Peeters,Slim Essid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出S-SONDO框架，首个仅利用输出嵌入向量对通用音频基础模型进行知识蒸馏的方法，无需logits或层级别对齐，架构无关，可将模型压缩61倍的同时保留96%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频基础模型参数量巨大（数亿级），推理成本高，难以部署在边缘设备上。已有的知识蒸馏方法大多局限于监督学习场景，依赖类别logits、中间特征或特定架构技术，无法处理仅输出嵌入向量的模型（如自监督或度量学习模型）。

Method: S-SONDO框架仅利用教师模型的输出嵌入向量进行知识蒸馏，无需logits或层级别对齐，因而架构无关且广泛适用于基于嵌入的教师模型。文中还探讨了损失函数选择和基于聚类的平衡数据采样等实用技巧。

Result: 将两个音频基础模型蒸馏为三个高效学生模型，模型大小缩小最多61倍，同时保留教师模型高达96%的性能。

Conclusion: S-SONDO是首个面向通用音频基础模型的仅基于输出嵌入向量的自监督知识蒸馏框架，有效解决了大模型压缩问题，且具有架构通用性。

Abstract: General audio foundation models have recently achieved remarkable progress, enabling strong performance across diverse tasks. However, state-of-the-art models remain extremely large, often with hundreds of millions of parameters, leading to high inference costs and limited deployability on edge devices. Knowledge distillation is a proven strategy for model compression, but prior work in audio has mostly focused on supervised settings, relying on class logits, intermediate features, or architecture-specific techniques. Such assumptions exclude models that output only embeddings, such as self-supervised or metric-learning models. We introduce S-SONDO (Self-Supervised KnOwledge DistillatioN for General AuDio FOundation Models), the first framework to distill general audio models using only their output embeddings. By avoiding the need for logits or layer-level alignment, S-SONDO is architecture-agnostic and broadly applicable to embedding-based teachers. We demonstrate its effectiveness by distilling two audio foundation models into three efficient students that are up to 61 times smaller while retaining up to 96% of teacher performance. We also provide practical insights on loss choice and clustering-based balanced data sampling. Code is available here: https://github.com/MedAliAdlouni/ssondo.

</details>


### [77] [Adaptive Prompt Embedding Optimization for LLM Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24983)
*Miles Q. Li,Benjamin C. M. Fung,Boyang Li,Radin Hamidi Rad,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Prompt嵌入优化(PEO)方法，直接在原始提示词的嵌入空间进行多轮白盒越狱攻击优化，无需添加对抗性token，即可保持原提示文本不变并实现攻击成功率超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白盒越狱攻击通常对用户提示追加离散对抗性后缀，这会显著改变提示文本且在组合性token空间中操作。前人工作避免直接优化原始提示token的嵌入，担心破坏语义内容。本文旨在证明该担忧不成立，并开发更有效的越狱方法。

Method: PEO采用连续嵌入空间优化，结合结构化延续目标(continuation targets)和自适应失败聚焦调度(adaptive failure-focused schedule)。多轮优化中利用启发式复合响应支架(heuristic composite response scaffolds)，并通过最近token投影保证可见提示文本完全不变。

Result: 在两个标准有害行为基准测试中，PEO在实验中优于所有竞争的变体白盒攻击方法，包括离散后缀搜索、追加对抗性嵌入和基于搜索的对抗生成。ASR-Judge验证性能提升并非仅来自空格式或支架输出。

Conclusion: 直接优化原始提示token的嵌入是可行且有效的，优化的嵌入保持接近原始嵌入，模型回答在绝大部分提示中保持主题相关性，打破了此前认为会破坏语义的顾虑。

Abstract: Existing white-box jailbreak attacks against aligned LLMs typically append discrete adversarial suffixes to the user prompt, which visibly alters the prompt and operates in a combinatorial token space. Prior work has avoided directly optimizing the embeddings of the original prompt tokens, presumably because perturbing them risks destroying the prompt's semantic content. We propose Prompt Embedding Optimization (PEO), a multi-round white-box jailbreak that directly optimizes the embeddings of the original prompt tokens without appending any adversarial tokens, and show that the concern is unfounded: the optimized embeddings remain close enough to their originals that the visible prompt string is preserved exactly after nearest-token projection, and quantitative analysis shows the model's responses stay on topic for the large majority of prompts. PEO combines continuous embedding-space optimization with structured continuation targets and an adaptive failure-focused schedule. Counterintuitively, later PEO rounds can benefit from heuristic composite response scaffolds that are not natural standalone templates, yet ASR-Judge shows that the resulting gains are not merely empty formatting or scaffold-only outputs. Across two standard harmful-behavior benchmarks and competing white-box attacks spanning discrete suffix search, appended adversarial embeddings, and search-based adversarial generation, PEO outperforms all of them in our experiments.

</details>


### [78] [Assessing Y-Axis Influence: Bias in Multimodal Language Models on Chart-to-Table Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24987)
*Seok Hwan Song,Azher Ahmed Efat,Wallapak Tavanapong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FairChart2Table框架，系统分析图表到表格翻译任务中y轴信息相关偏见对多模态语言模型性能的影响，发现数字长度、刻度数量、数值范围和刻度格式等y轴特征存在显著偏倚，且图例数量也会影响模型性能，而通过提示注入y轴信息可有效提升部分模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表数据集在y轴信息的多个维度上存在图像数量不均衡，导致多模态语言模型学习时产生非预期的偏倚，影响其在复杂查询上的性能，而此前工作未系统性地研究这些偏倚。

Method: 提出FairChart2Table框架，在五个SOTA多模态语言模型上系统分析主刻度值数字长度、主刻度数量、数值范围、刻度值格式（如缩写或科学计数法）以及图例/实体数量等因素带来的y轴相关偏倚。

Result: (1) 主刻度值数字长度、主刻度数量、数值范围和刻度值格式均存在显著y轴偏倚；(2) 图表中图例/实体数量影响MLM性能；(3) 向MLM提供y轴信息提示可显著提升部分模型的性能。

Conclusion: 图表到表格翻译中y轴信息偏倚是真实存在且影响模型性能的关键因素，提示工程可缓解部分偏倚，为构建更公平的图表理解系统提供了方向。

Abstract: Chart-to-table translation converts chart images into structured tabular data. Accurate translation is crucial for Multimodal Language Model (MLM) to answer complex queries. We observe imbalances in the number of images across different aspects of the y-axis information in public chart datasets. Such imbalances can introduce unintended biases, causing uneven MLM performance. Previous works have not systematically examined these biases. To address this gap, we propose a new framework, FairChart2Table, for analyzing y-axis-related bias on five state-of-the-art models. Key Findings: (1) There are significant y-axis biases related to the digit length of the major tick values, the number of major ticks, the range of values, and the tick value format (e.g., abbreviation or scientific format). (2) The number of legends/entities in chart images impacts MLM performance. (3) Prompting MLM with y-axis information can significantly enhance the performance for some MLMs.

</details>


### [79] [Sparse Personalized Text Generation with Multi-Trajectory Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24996)
*Bo Ni,Haowei Fu,Qinwen Ge,Franck Dernoncourt,Samyadeep Basu,Nedim Lipka,Seunghyun Yoon,Yu Wang,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Puneet Mathur,Ryan A. Rossi,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAT是一个针对冷启动场景的LLM个性化推理框架，通过检索写作风格和主题偏好两条互补轨迹，并采用强化学习迭代双推理机制融合信号，在稀疏数据条件下显著提升生成质量和对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法依赖密集交互历史，在冷启动场景（数据稀疏或不可用）下效果不佳；外部信号虽可提供补救但原始上下文噪声大，现有方法难以对异构数据源进行推理。

Method: 提出PAT框架：1) 沿两条互补轨迹检索信息——风格相似用户的写作风格线索和偏好对齐用户的主题特定上下文；2) 采用基于强化学习的迭代双推理机制，使LLM联合优化并整合这两类信号。

Result: 在真实世界个性化基准测试上，PAT在稀疏数据条件下持续提升了生成质量和用户对齐效果。

Conclusion: PAT为冷启动个性化问题提供了一个强解决方案，验证了利用强化学习融合异构信号进行推理的有效性。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) advance, personalization has become a key mechanism for tailoring outputs to individual user needs. However, most existing methods rely heavily on dense interaction histories, making them ineffective in cold-start scenarios where such data is sparse or unavailable. While external signals (e.g., content of similar users) can offer a potential remedy, leveraging them effectively remains challenging: raw context is often noisy, and existing methods struggle to reason over heterogeneous data sources. To address these issues, we introduce PAT (Personalization with Aligned Trajectories), a reasoning framework for cold-start LLM personalization. PAT first retrieves information along two complementary trajectories: writing-style cues from stylistically similar users and topic-specific context from preference-aligned users. It then employs a reinforcement learning-based, iterative dual-reasoning mechanism that enables the LLM to jointly refine and integrate these signals. Experimental results across real-world personalization benchmarks show that PAT consistently improves generation quality and alignment under sparse-data conditions, establishing a strong solution to the cold-start personalization problem.

</details>


### [80] [Evaluating Risks in Weak-to-Strong Alignment: A Bias-Variance Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25077)
*Hamid Osooli,Kareema Batool,Rick Gentry,Tiasa Singha Roy,Ashwin Gupta,Anirudha Ramesh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过偏差-方差-协方差视角分析了弱到强对齐中的盲点欺骗问题，发现强模型方差是欺骗行为的最强预测指标，协方差提供额外但较弱的信息，并建议将强模型方差作为早期预警信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱到强对齐在弱教师盲点区域可能失败，导致强模型自信地犯错；现有研究缺乏对置信度和不确定性分布的细粒度分析，需要超越聚合精度来理解失败机制。

Method: 采用偏差-方差-协方差分解框架，推导了基于失配的弱到强总体风险上界；使用连续置信度分数分析经验成分；在PKU-SafeRLHF和HH-RLHF数据集上评估了SFT、RLHF和RLAIF四种弱到强流程，并设计了盲点欺骗度量指标。

Result: 强模型方差是盲点欺骗的最强经验预测指标；协方差提供额外但较弱的信息；弱-强依赖关系本身不足以解释观察到的失败。

Conclusion: 强模型方差可作为弱到强欺骗的早期预警信号；盲点评估有助于区分失败是继承自弱监督还是源于弱模型不确定区域。

Abstract: Weak-to-strong alignment offers a promising route to scalable supervision, but it can fail when a strong model becomes confidently wrong on examples that lie in the weak teacher's blind spots. Understanding such failures requires going beyond aggregate accuracy, since weak-to-strong errors depend not only on whether the strong model disagrees with its teacher, but also on how confidence and uncertainty are distributed across examples. In this work, we analyze weak-to-strong alignment through a bias-variance-covariance lens that connects misfit theory to practical post-training pipelines. We derive a misfit-based upper bound on weak-to-strong population risk and study its empirical components using continuous confidence scores. We evaluate four weak-to-strong pipelines spanning supervised fine-tuning (SFT), reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), and reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF) on the PKU-SafeRLHF and HH-RLHF datasets. Using a blind-spot deception metric that isolates cases where the strong model is confidently wrong while the weak model is uncertain, we find that strong-model variance is the strongest empirical predictor of deception across our settings. Covariance provides additional but weaker information, indicating that weak-strong dependence matters, but does not by itself explain the observed failures. These results suggest that strong-model variance can serve as an early-warning signal for weak-to-strong deception, while blind-spot evaluation helps distinguish whether failures are inherited from weak supervision or arise in regions of weak-model uncertainty.

</details>


### [81] [Cooperate to Compete: Strategic Coordination in Multi-Agent Conquest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25088)
*Abigail O'Neill,Alan Zhu,Mihran Miroyan,Narges Norouzi,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C2C多智能体环境，研究LM基智能体在混合动机场景（短期合作与长期竞争并存）中的谈判行为。通过AI-only游戏和人类-AI对比实验，发现人类谈判行为与LM基智能体存在显著差异：人类偏好低复杂度交易、更不可靠、谈判更具攻击性。基于这些发现改进提示词后，智能体胜率从22.2%提升至32.7%。实验包含1100+场游戏、16000+次对话、1520万token和15万+动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LM基智能体在混合动机场景（如多方政治博弈）中尚未得到充分测试，这类场景要求智能体利用短期合作实现长期竞争目标。

Method: 设计C2C多智能体环境，玩家拥有非对称目标和非约束性谈判（联盟可形成亦可破裂）；进行AI-only游戏和人类vs AI用户研究；通过定向提示工程改进智能体谈判行为。

Result: 人类与LM智能体存在显著行为差异：人类接受无还价交易比例为56.3%（智能体67.6%）；人类是更不可靠的合作伙伴；改进提示后智能体胜率从22.2%提升至32.7%。

Conclusion: C2C可作为研究和构建LM基智能体的测试平台，帮助其掌握现实部署所需的复杂协调能力。

Abstract: Language Model (LM)-based agents remain largely untested in mixed-motive settings where agents must leverage short-term cooperation for long-term competitive goals (e.g., multi-party politics). We introduce Cooperate to Compete (C2C), a multi-agent environment where players can engage in private negotiations while competing to be the first to achieve their secret objective. Players have asymmetric objectives and negotiations are non-binding, allowing alliances to form and break as players' short-term interests align and diverge. We run AI only games and conduct a user study pitting human players against AI opponents. We identify significant differences between human and AI negotiation behaviors, finding that humans favor lower-complexity deals and are significantly less reliable partners compared to LM-based agents. We also find that humans are more aggressive negotiators, accepting deals without a counteroffer only 56.3% of the time compared to 67.6% for LM-based agents. Through targeted prompting inspired by these findings, we modify agents' negotiation behavior and improve win rates from 22.2% to 32.7%. We run over 1,100 games with over 16,000 private conversations totaling 15.2 million tokens and over 150,000 player actions. Our results establish C2C as a testbed for studying and building LM-based agents that can navigate the sophisticated coordination required for real-world deployments. The game, code, and dataset may be found at https://negotiationgame.io/c2c.

</details>


### [82] [Doing More With Less: Revisiting the Effectiveness of LLM Pruning for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25098)
*Ocean Monjur,Shahriar Kabir Nahin,Anshuman Chhabra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现非结构化剪枝（移除冗余权重）反而能增强推理型大语言模型在测试时计算扩展（TTS）中的表现，甚至有时优于未剪枝的完整模型，挑战了“剪枝必然降低TTS性能”的传统观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为结构化剪枝（移除整层模块）会严重损害推理型LLM的TTS性能，但非结构化剪枝（仅移除冗余权重）是否也有类似局限尚未被充分探索。本文旨在重新审视这一假设。

Method: 在s1.1-7B和Qwen3-8B两个推理型LLM上，针对四个推理基准，系统比较结构化剪枝与非结构化剪枝对TTS性能的影响，并进一步研究不同层间稀疏度分配策略的作用。

Result: 非结构化剪枝相比结构化剪枝能一致地提升TTS性能，且有时甚至超越原始未剪枝的全权重模型。层间稀疏度分配策略对剪枝效果有重要影响。

Conclusion: 挑战了传统认知，表明精心设计的剪枝（尤其是非结构化剪枝）不仅可以不损害TTS性能，甚至能进一步提升推理效率。

Abstract: While current Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable reasoning capabilities through test-time compute scaling (TTS), their massive parameter counts and high inference costs have motivated the development of pruning methods that can reduce model size without sacrificing performance. However, specific to reasoning LLMs, prior work has shown that structured pruning (methods which removes entire set of layer blocks), significantly degrades TTS reasoning performance. In this work, we revisit this assumption and instead investigate whether unstructured pruning (methods that carefully remove only certain redundant/detrimental weights) exhibits similar limitations. Surprisingly, our extensive experiments across four reasoning benchmarks on two reasoning LLMs: s1.1-7B and Qwen3-8B, consistently show that unstructured pruning augments TTS performance compared to structured pruning, and at times can even outperform the unpruned full-weight LLMs. Furthermore, we also empirically study the impact of different layer-wise sparsity allocation strategies, which are an important parametric choice for instantiating unstructured pruning methods. These findings challenge the conventional notion that pruning always reduces TTS performance and in fact, suggest that carefully undertaken pruning can improve TTS effectiveness even further.

</details>


### [83] [Semantic Layers for Reliable LLM-Powered Data Analytics: A Paired Benchmark of Accuracy and Hallucination Across Three Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25149)
*Michael Rumiantsau,Ivan Fokeev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明，为大语言模型提供显式的业务语义层文档（而非仅依赖数据库模式）可将文本到SQL的查询准确率提升17-23个百分点，且模型间的差异被语义层覆盖，表明错误根源在于模型被迫推断模式未编码的业务语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自然语言查询分析数据库时存在两个交织的失败模式——错误回答和自信幻觉，两者根源相同：模型被迫推断模式未编码的业务语义。本文旨在检验提供这些语义作为上下文是否能弥合这一差距。

Method: 在ClickHouse上使用清理后的Contoso零售数据集，对三个前沿LLM（Claude Opus 4.7、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.4）进行100个自然语言问题的基准测试。采用配对单次协议，每个模型评估两次：仅给仓库模式，以及提供模式加4KB手写标记文档（描述数据集度量、约定和消歧规则）。

Result: 添加语义文档后，三个模型的准确率提升17-23个百分点。有文档时模型间无显著差异（67.7-68.7%），无文档时也无差异（45.5-50.5%）。所有跨簇比较p<0.01显著。语义层文档解释了几乎所有显著方差，模型选择在层级内无显著影响。

Conclusion: 显式业务语义通过改变模型被要求执行的任务的本质（而非提升模型能力）来抑制文本到SQL的主要错误类别。这是一个结构性结果：关键因素在于是否提供语义上下文，而非选择哪个模型。

Abstract: LLMs deployed for natural-language querying of analytical databases suffer from two intertwined failures - incorrect answers and confident hallucinations - both rooted in the same cause: the model is forced to infer business semantics that the schema does not encode. We test whether supplying those semantics as context closes the gap. We benchmark three frontier LLMs (Claude Opus 4.7, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.4) on 100 natural-language questions over the Cleaned Contoso Retail Dataset in ClickHouse, using a paired single-shot protocol. Each model is evaluated twice: once given only the warehouse schema, and once given the schema plus a 4 KB hand-authored markdown document describing the dataset's measures, conventions, and disambiguation rules. Adding the document improves accuracy by +17 to +23 percentage points across all three models. With it, the three models are statistically indistinguishable (67.7-68.7%); without it, they are also indistinguishable (45.5-50.5%). Every cross-cluster comparison is significant at p < 0.01. The presence of the semantic-layer document accounts for essentially all of the significant variance; model choice within tier does not. We interpret this as a structural result: explicit business semantics suppress the dominant class of text-to-SQL errors not by making the model more capable, but by changing what the model is being asked to do.

</details>


### [84] [Training Transformers as a Universal Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25166)
*Ruize Xu,Chenxiao Yang,Yanhong Li,David McAllester*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个小型Transformer可以通过在简化但图灵完备的编程语言MicroPy上训练，学会执行程序（包括人类编写的多种程序），并在分布外泛化上表现出色，为Transformer可作为通用计算机提供了实证证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标准Transformer架构是否可以被训练成通用计算机，即在图灵完备的编程语言上执行任意程序。

Method: 使用小型Transformer在MicroPy语言上训练，采用PENCIL脚手架技术在有限上下文窗口内实现空间高效的小步执行。训练数据为随机生成的无意义MicroPy程序，测试时评估包括位复制/翻转、二进制加减法、SAT验证/求解等人类编写的程序。

Result: 训练后的Transformer能够泛化到多种人类编写的程序，并实现了分布外泛化，即能够评估来自训练分布之外的新程序。

Conclusion: 由于MicroPy可以表达任意计算，该结果为标准Transformer可以被训练成通用计算机提供了实证依据。

Abstract: We demonstrate that a small transformer can learn to execute programs in MicroPy, a simplified yet computationally universal programming language. Given procedure definitions together with an expression to evaluate, the transformer predicts small-step execution using PENCIL scaffolding for space-efficient execution within a bounded context window. After training on randomly generated, meaningless MicroPy programs, the learned transformer generalizes to various human-written programs including bit copying and flipping, binary addition and multiplication, and SAT verification and solving. We note that the trained model can achieve out-of-distribution generalization; i.e., evaluate novel programs from distribution on programs. Since MicroPy can express any computation, our results provide empirical evidence that a standard transformer can be trained to act as a universal computer.

</details>


### [85] [From Insight to Action: A Novel Framework for Interpretability-Guided Data Selection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25167)
*Ling Shi,Xinwei Wu,Xiaohu Zhao,Hao Wang,Heng Liu,Yangyang Liu,Linlong Xu,Longyue Wang,Deyi Xiong,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可解释性引导的数据选择框架（IGDS），通过识别模型内部因果任务特征并选取能最大激活这些特征的数据进行微调，在数学推理、摘要和翻译任务上显著提升了数据效率，例如在Gemma-2-2B上仅用50%数据就超越全数据微调17.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释性工具（如稀疏自编码器）能够揭示LLM内部的有意义特征，但将这些洞察转化为模型优化的实际操作方法仍存在关键空白。本文旨在弥合这一差距，验证"基于模型内部任务特征指导数据选择是有效训练策略"这一假设。

Method: IGDS框架分为两步：(1) 通过频率召回和干预滤波识别因果任务特征；(2) 选取能够最大化激活这些任务特征的"特征共振数据"用于微调。

Result: 在Gemma-2、LLaMA-3.1和Qwen3模型上的数学推理、摘要和翻译任务中，IGDS展现出卓越的数据效率。在数学任务上，IGDS仅用50%数据就在Gemma-2-2B上超越全数据微调17.4%，且优于基于数据质量和多样性的基线方法。分析证实特征放大与任务性能提升之间存在强正相关。

Conclusion: IGDS提供了一个直接且有效的框架，通过利用LLM内部机制来增强模型性能，验证了核心假设的正确性。

Abstract: While mechanistic interpretability tools like Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) can uncover meaningful features within Large Language Models (LLMs), a critical gap remains in transforming these insights into practical actions for model optimization. We bridge this gap with the hypothesis that data selection guided by a model's internal task features is a effective training strategy. Inspired by this, we propose Interpretability-Guided Data Selection (IGDS), a framework that first identifies these causal task features through frequency recall and interventional filtering, then selects ``Feature-Resonant Data'' that maximally activates task features for fine-tuning. We validate IGDS on mathematical reasoning, summarization, and translation tasks within Gemma-2, LLaMA-3.1, and Qwen3 models. Our experiments demonstrate exceptional data efficiency: on the Math task, IGDS surpasses full-dataset fine-tuning by a remarkable 17.4% on Gemma-2-2B while using only 50% of the data, and outperforms established baselines focused on data quality and diversity. Analysis confirms a strong positive correlation between feature amplification and task performance improvement. IGDS thus provides a direct and effective framework to enhance LLMs by leveraging their internal mechanisms, validating our core hypothesis.

</details>


### [86] [DATAREEL: Automated Data-Driven Video Story Generation with Animations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25220)
*Ridwan Mahbub,Syem Aziz,Mahir Ahmed,Shadikur Rahman,Mizanur Rahman,Shafiq Joty,Enamul Hoque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DataReel基准（328个真实数据故事），用于评估自动生成动画数据视频故事的能力，并设计了一个多智能体框架（规划-生成-验证三阶段），实验表明该框架优于直接提示基线，但仍面临动画、叙述与视觉强调协调的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据视频作为一种结合动画图表与旁白的强大叙事媒介，在新闻、教育和公共传播中应用广泛，但生成过程需要专业知识且自动化缺乏统一评估标准。大语言模型虽有自动化潜力，却缺少针对动画可视化视频故事生成的基准测试。

Method: 构建DataReel基准（328个真实数据故事，包含结构化数据、图表可视化和旁白文本）；提出多智能体框架，将任务分解为规划（planning）、生成（generation）和验证（verification）三个阶段，模拟人类故事创作流程。

Result: 多智能体框架在自动评估和人工评估中均优于直接提示基线；实验揭示了动画、旁白和视觉强调之间协调仍存在持续性挑战。

Conclusion: DataReel为数据驱动视频故事生成提供了首个标准化评估基准，多智能体框架展示了自动化潜力，但动画-旁白-视觉的协同仍是未来研究重点。

Abstract: Data videos are a powerful medium for visual data based storytelling, combining animated, chart-centric visualizations with synchronized narration. Widely used in journalism, education, and public communication, they help audiences understand complex data through clear and engaging visual explanations. Despite their growing impact, generating data-driven video stories remains challenging, as it requires careful coordination of visual encoding, temporal progression, and narration and substantial expertise in visualization design, animation, and video-editing tools. Recent advances in large language models offer new opportunities to automate this process; however, there is currently no benchmark for rigorously evaluating models on animated visualization-based video storytelling. To address this gap, we introduce DataReel, a benchmark for automated data-driven video story generation comprising 328 real-world stories. Each story pairs structured data, a chart visualization, and a narration transcript, enabling systematic evaluation of models' abilities to generate animated data video stories. We further propose a multi-agent framework that decomposes the task into planning, generation, and verification stages, mirroring key aspects of the human storytelling process. Experiments show that this multi-agent approach outperforms direct prompting baselines under both automatic and human evaluations, while revealing persistent challenges in coordinating animation, narration, and visual emphasis. We release DataReel at https://github.com/vis-nlp/DataReel.

</details>


### [87] [ValueAlpha: Agreement-Gated Stress Testing of LLM-Judged Investment Rationales Before Returns Are Observable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25224)
*Sidi Chang,Peiying Zhu,Yuxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ValueAlpha 是一个预注册的一致性门控压力测试协议，用于决定 LLM 评判的投资理由主张是否可发布。在1100条轨迹、5500次评判调用中，整体一致性通过（κ̄_w=0.7168），但发现低级系统陷入平局、约束感知维度未通过（κ̄_w=0.2022）、简洁正确理由被扣分（Δ=-2.81）等问题。该协议不是一个排行榜或投资能力测量工具，而是一个前校准计量层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期投资决策存在"预实现评估问题"：已实现收益作为投资质量的最终仲裁者，但到来太晚且噪声过大，无法指导模型开发和治理决策。LLM评判看似可以替代部署前评估，但未经验证的评判者可能奖励冗长、自信或模仿评分标准的行为，而非真正的金融判断能力。

Method: 提出 ValueAlpha 协议：(1) 预注册协议设计；(2) 一致性门控（agreement-gated）机制，设置整体和分维度的加权一致性阈值；(3) 包含1000个诚实决策周期和100个预注册对抗性控制（共1100条轨迹，5500次评判调用）；(4) 采用目标锚点特异性探测（targeted anchor-specificity probe）检验金融构念的操作性负载。

Result: (1) 整体一致性通过门控（κ̄_w=0.7168）；(2) 低级系统在平局类中无法区分；(3) constraint_awareness 维度未通过门控（κ̄_w=0.2022）；(4) 单一评判者排名具有家族依赖性；(5) 简洁正确理由比诚实理由多扣2.81个评分点；(6) 锚点特异性探测表明约束感知等金融构念具有操作性负载。

Conclusion: ValueAlpha 不是排行榜，也不声称能测量真实投资技能。它是一个前校准计量层（pre-calibration metrology layer），用于判断提出的 LLM 评判投资理由主张是否足够稳定、一致且无污染，以决定是否可以报告。该协议解决了 LLM 作为评判者时的可信度问题，提供了在部署前评估 AI 金融系统的标准化方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon investment decisions create a pre-realization evaluation problem: realized returns are the eventual arbiter of investment quality, but they arrive too late and are too noisy to guide many model-development and governance decisions. LLM judges offer a tempting substitute for pre-deployment evaluation of AI-finance systems, but unvalidated judges may reward verbosity, confidence, or rubric mimicry rather than financial judgment. This paper introduces \textbf{ValueAlpha}, a preregistered agreement-gated stress-test protocol for deciding when LLM-judged investment-rationale claims are publishable, qualified, or invalid.
  In a controlled market-state capital-allocation prototype with 1,000 honest decision cycles and 100 preregistered adversarial controls (1,100 trajectories, 5,500 judge calls), ValueAlpha clears the aggregate agreement gate at \(\barκ_w = 0.7168\) but prevents several overclaims. Lower-rank systems collapse into a tie-class, one rubric dimension fails the per-dimension gate (\texttt{constraint\_awareness}, \(\barκ_w = 0.2022\)), single-judge rankings are family-dependent, and terse-correct rationales receive a \(Δ= -2.81\) rubric-point penalty relative to honest rationales. A targeted anchor-specificity probe further shows that financial constructs such as constraint awareness are operationally load-bearing.
  The contribution is therefore not a leaderboard and not a claim to measure true investment skill. ValueAlpha is a pre-calibration metrology layer for AI-finance evaluation: it determines whether a proposed LLM-judge-based investment-rationale claim is stable enough, agreed enough, and uncontaminated enough to be reported at all.

</details>


### [88] [JURY-RL: Votes Propose, Proofs Dispose for Label-Free RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25419)
*Xinjie Chen,Biao Fu,Jing Wu,Guoxin Chen,Xinggao Liu,Dayiheng Liu,Minpeng Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JURY-RL是一个无标签的RLVR框架，通过将答案提议与奖励处置解耦，仅对通过Lean形式验证器验证的多数投票答案给予奖励，并在验证不确定时使用ResZero回退机制分配零均值、方差保持的信号，从而在无需人工标注的情况下稳定地训练LLM推理，在数学推理基准上达到与有监督真实标签训练相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR依赖人工标注答案或精心设计的奖励规范，成本高昂；无标签替代方法（如多数投票或LLM-as-a-judge）虽去除了标注成本，但引入了假阳性问题，导致训练不稳定。因此需要一种无标签、可验证的奖励框架，既能消除标注成本，又能避免假阳性带来的训练不稳定。

Method: JURY-RL将答案提议与奖励处置解耦：模型rollout投票产生候选答案，Lean形式验证器决定该候选答案是否能获得正奖励。只有匹配多数投票答案且通过Lean验证的rollout才获得奖励；当验证不确定时，采用ResZero（残差零化）回退机制，丢弃未验证的多数提案，在剩余答案上重新分配零均值、方差保持的信号，以维持稳定的优化梯度。

Result: 在三个骨干模型上，JURY-RL在数学推理基准上持续优于其他无标签基线，并具备竞争力的代码生成和通用基准迁移能力。在pass@1指标上与有监督真实标签训练相当，且pass@k更高、响应多样性更优，展现了更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: JURY-RL通过解耦答案提议与奖励验证，结合形式验证器和ResZero回退机制，实现了无需人工标注的稳定RLVR训练，在推理任务上达到甚至超越有监督方法的性能水平。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) enhances the reasoning of large language models (LLMs), but standard RLVR often depends on human-annotated answers or carefully curated reward specifications. In machine-checkable domains, label-free alternatives such as majority voting or LLM-as-a-judge remove annotation cost but can introduce false positives that destabilize training. We introduce JURY-RL, a label-free RLVR framework that decouples answer proposal from reward disposal: votes from model rollouts propose a candidate answer, and a formal verifier determines whether that candidate can receive positive reward. Concretely, only rollouts matching the plurality-voted answer are rewarded when that answer is successfully verified in Lean. When verification is inconclusive, we invoke ResZero (Residual-Zero), a fallback reward that discards the unverified plurality proposal and redistributes a zero-mean, variance-preserving signal over the residual answers. This design maintains a stable optimization gradient without reinforcing unverifiable consensus. Across three backbone models trained on mathematical data, JURY-RL consistently outperforms other label-free baselines on mathematical reasoning benchmarks and transfers competitively to code generation and general benchmarks. It attains pass@1 performance comparable to supervised ground-truth training, with superior generalization demonstrated by higher pass@k and response diversity.

</details>


### [89] [PI-TTA: Physics-Informed Source-Free Test-Time Adaptation for Robust Human Activity Recognition on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25435)
*Changyu Li,Lu Wang,Ming Lei,Jiashen Liu,Yichen Zhang,Kaishun Wu,Fei Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PI-TTA，一种面向移动感知的物理约束源自由测试时自适应框架，通过重力一致性、短时时间连续性和频谱稳定性三个物理约束，解决了传感器流式非独立同分布数据下的不稳定自适应问题，在三个HAR数据集上提升了长期序列准确率并降低了物理违例率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉领域的测试时自适应方法直接应用于传感器人体活动识别时，因行为惯性流数据存在时间相关性和传感器旋转、放置变化、采样率漂移等会话内偏移，导致置信度过高错误、表征坍塌和灾难性遗忘等严重退化问题。

Method: 提出PI-TTA框架，包含三个物理一致性约束：重力一致性（利用重力方向先验）、短时时间连续性（相邻时刻信号平滑性）和频谱稳定性（频域特征保持），仅更新少量参数子集，适用于设备端部署。

Result: 在USCHAD、PAMAP2和mHealth数据集上，长期序列准确率提升最高9.13%，物理违例率分别降低27.5%、24.1%和45.4%，且仅引入适度计算开销。

Conclusion: 物理信息引导的自适应能够提升移动传感系统在真实流式场景下的准确性、稳定性和部署可靠性。

Abstract: Source-free test-time adaptation (TTA) is appealing for mobile and wearable sensing because it enables on-device personalization from unlabeled test streams without centralizing private data. However, sensor-based human activity recognition (HAR) poses challenges that are less pronounced in standard vision benchmarks: behavioral inertial streams are temporally correlated and often exhibit within-session shifts caused by sensor rotation, placement change, and sampling-rate drift. Under this streaming non-i.i.d. setting, widely used vision-style TTA objectives can become unstable, leading to overconfident errors, representation collapse, and catastrophic forgetting. We propose PI-TTA, a lightweight source-free adaptation framework that stabilizes online updates through three physics-consistent constraints: gravity consistency, short-horizon temporal continuity, and spectral stability. PI-TTA updates the same small parameter subset as strong source-free baselines and incurs only modest overhead, making it suitable for on-device deployment. Experiments on USCHAD, PAMAP2, and mHealth under long-sequence stress tests and factorized shift protocols show that PI-TTA mitigates the severe degradation observed in confidence-driven baselines and preserves stable adaptation under sustained streaming conditions. It improves long-sequence accuracy by up to 9.13% and reduces physical-violation rates by 27.5%, 24.1%, and 45.4% on USCHAD, PAMAP2, and mHealth, respectively. These results demonstrate that physics-informed adaptation can improve accuracy, stability, and deployment reliability for real-world mobile sensing systems.

</details>


### [90] [SciEval: A Benchmark for Automatic Evaluation of K-12 Science Instructional Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25472)
*Zhaohui Li,Peng He,Zhiyuan Chen,Honglu Liu,Zeyuan Wang,Tingting Li,Jinjun Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了自动教学材料评估（AIME）任务，构建了基于EQuIP量表的SciEval基准数据集（273份教学材料、13个评估标准、N=3549），测试了多种主流LLM在该任务上的表现，发现通用LLM性能不佳，而基于Qwen3的领域微调可获得最高11%的性能提升，强调了领域特定微调在AIME任务中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着越来越多的教育工作者使用生成式AI创建教学材料，对K-12科学教育教学材料的评估需求日益增长。然而，人工审核教学材料耗时、依赖专业知识且难以规模化，因此迫切需要自动化的评估方法。

Method: 将自动教学材料评估（AIME）形式化为一个生成式AI任务，要求模型根据教育工作者设计的评分量规预测分数并提供证据。构建了SciEval基准数据集（基于EQuIP量表，273份教学材料，13个标准，共3549条评估），并测试了GPT、Gemini、Llama、Qwen等主流LLM，最后对Qwen3进行了领域微调。

Result: 主流LLM在SciEval上均未取得强性能表现；对Qwen3进行领域微调后，在保留测试集上实现了最高11%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 领域特定的微调对于AIME任务至关重要，本工作的数据集和基线模型可为LLM在教育任务中的应用提供支持。

Abstract: The need to evaluate instructional materials for K-12 science education has become increasingly important, as more educators use generative AI to create instructional materials. However, the review of instructional materials is time-consuming, expertise-intensive, and difficult to scale, motivating interest in automated evaluation approaches.
